- **service-de-channel-activate**: Activate an Enhanced `MessagingChannel` (WhatsApp/Apple/Facebook/SMS/RCS) by PATCHing `MessagingChannelUsage.DeploymentStatus` from `Disabled` to `Provisioning` via the REST sobject endpoint.
